Let's analyze mathematical structure.

We have nonnegative vector a length n, sum S. Objective f(a)=2n max_k c_k/S^2. Let normalize S=1. Let M=(n/(2n)) target? Actually target score T => max c = T/(2n). For n=6962, T=1.5027 => max c ≈ 1.5027/(13924)=1.0792e-4. Uniform center 1/n=1.436e-4. Need 25% reduction.

Convolution polynomial A(z)^2 coefficients. Nonnegative coefficients. Need minimize maximum coefficient given sum coefficients =1. def fft_len(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
def conv_full(a):
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1; L=next_pow2(m)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); return np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:m]
```

Objective for softmax fixed last:
```
def softmax_from_x(x, n):
    y = np.empty(n); y[:n-1]=x; y[-1]=0.0
    m = y.max()
    w = np.exp(y-m)
    # if n-1 maybe y[-1]=0 already included
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
But if x large >700, overflow before subtract? y-m stable if y finite. Use np.exp(y-m). If x contains inf from log(floor) no.

Objective gradient:
```
def smooth_score_grad(x, beta):
    n = len(a) # pass n? top-level can use global? Better arguments.
    a = softmax_from_x(x)
    n=len(a)
    M=2*n-1
    L=1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length() # Need enough for gradient corr length 3n-2
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
    t=n*c
    tmax=t.max()
    shifted=beta*(t-tmax)
    # exp maybe overflow if beta large, shifted≤0
    exp=np.exp(shifted)
    exp_sum=exp.sum()
    val=tmax + np.log(exp_sum)/beta
    p=exp/exp_sum
    # grad_a = 2*n*corr(p,a)
    r=p[::-1]
    R=np.fft.rfft(r,L) # L enough for conv length n+M-1=3n-2
    H=np.fft.irfft(A*R,L)
    q=H[n-2 : M+1][::-1]  # length n
    ga=2.0*n*q
    s=np.dot(a, ga)
    gx=a*(ga - s)
    # only first n-1
    gx=gx[:n-1]
    return val, gx
```
Check slice: H length L, valid conv indices 0..n+M-2 =3n-3. Need q j=0..n-1 index M-1-j from M-n to M-1. M=2n-1? Wait number of c coefficients = 2n-1, index 0..2n-2. Let K=2n-1. M maybe call K. In earlier I used M=2n-2 max index. Let's set K=2*n-1, last=K-1=2n-2. p_rev length K. h length n+K-1 =3n-2 indices 0..3n-3. q_j = h[last - j]. For j=0 -> h[last]; j=n-1 -> h[last-(n-1)] = h[n-1]? Because last=2n-2, minus n-1 = n-1. So slice h[n-1:last+1] length n, reverse. Example n=3 K=5 last=4, h[n-1=2:5] reverse [h4,h3,h2]. Good. Earlier I had n-2 due M definition off. So use start=n-1.
Need L >= 3n-2. `L = 1 << (3*n - 2 - 1).bit_length()` = next pow >=3n-2.
For c, using same L fine.

But is gradient of val multiple of score? val ≈ max t, score=2val. L-BFGS minimizes val. Good. For final exact, compute score.

Spectral projection can be run from parent with target M_target. Need implement without lambdas.

Spectral factorization projection function:
```
def spectral_project_dense(a, M_target, n_iters=100, L=None):
    a = positive normalize
    n=len(a); K=2*n-1; L=next_pow(3*n-2) maybe full FFT length K pow.
    best=a; bests=exact
    M = exact score/(2n)
    for it:
       A_full = np.fft.fft(a, L)
       C = A_full*A_full
       c = np.fft.ifft(C).real[:K]
       # water-fill c to M_target and sum1
       c_proj = c.copy()
       c_proj[c_proj > M_target] = M_target
       # Need sum1. If sum <1, raise uniformly until max M_target.
       s = c_proj.sum()
       if s < 1:
           # find theta such that sum(min(M_target, c+theta))=1 where c clipped? We can binary search theta in [0, M_target - min(c_clipped)]
           low=0; high=M_target - c_proj.min()
           for _ in range(50): mid=(low+high)/2; val=(np.minimum(M_target, c_proj+mid)).sum(); if val<1: low=mid else high=mid
           c_proj=np.minimum(M_target, c_proj+(low+high)/2)
       else:
           # If sum>1? Clipping only reduces, no.
       # normalize by sum just in case
       c_proj /= c_proj.sum()
       C_t = np.fft.fft(c_proj, L)
       # ensure C_t[0] ~1
       A_new = np.sqrt(C_t * (1.0 / C_t[0]))
       # But if C_t[0] complex? should be 1 real. Use real.
       a_new = np.fft.ifft(A_new).real[:n]
       # To improve nonnegativity, blend with a and maybe sign? 
       a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0)
       S=a_new.sum(); if S<=0: break
       a_new/=S
       sc=exact_score(a_new)
       # try blend factors
       for t in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]: a_blend=(1-t)*a + t*a_new; normalize; evaluate; accept best.
       # also try iterating on a_new if M_target same
    return best
```
But taking sqrt of C_t where C_t may be negative in some frequencies; np.sqrt complex yields complex. If A_new not exactly Hermitian, ifft complex; we take real. Should be okay if C_t Hermitian (real c_proj). `fft` of real c_proj returns Hermitian full spectrum. sqrt preserves if principal branch: sqrt(conj z)=conj sqrt(z). Yes. DC positive after normalize.
Will this produce a with c near c_proj? Initially C=A^2. We replace C with target C_t; sqrt gives A candidate whose square is C_t. Good. Time a_new may have negatives. Clipping/renormalizing changes. Alternating may converge.

Water-filling: If M_target too low (below lower bound), c_proj all M_target? Sum K M_target maybe >1 if M_target >1/K. Feasible. If M_target < c_min? high. Binary search okay.

We can start M = current max c. Decrease M: for factor in [0.98,0.95,0.9,0.8, target]. But if M_target below current by too much, water-fill c_proj may deviate far. Instead update M = max( M*0.95, target_M ). Iterate. Accept improvements.

def run_lbfgs_stages(a, deadline, max_seconds_per_stage=180):
    a=np.maximum(a,0); normalize
    best=a; bests=exact
    stages=[(10, 'LB10'), (30,...), (100), (300), (1000), (3000), (10000), (30000)]
    for beta,label in stages:
        if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
        maxfun = 3000 if len(a)<10000 else 1500? 
        res=minimize(softmax_obj_grad, x0, args=(n,beta,L), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':800,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-11})
        a_out=softmax_from_x(res.x)
        sc=exact_score(a_out)
        if sc<bests: update
        # maybe if no improvement, break high beta? continue lower
    return best
```

Let's code `softmax_obj_grad` carefully:
```
def softmax_obj_grad(x, n, beta, L):
    y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[:-1] = x
    y[-1] = 0.0
    m = np.max(y)
    w = np.exp(y - m)
    S = np.sum(w)
    if S <= 0: return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    a = w / S
    # c
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    K = 2*n - 1
    c = c[:K]
    # For numerical negative small, ignore? c should nonneg; clip to 0 maybe.
    if c.min() < 0: c = np.maximum(c, 0.0) # but this changes sum slightly
    t = n * c
    tmax = float(t.max())
    sh = beta * (t - tmax)
    # clip sh below -745 to avoid underflow? exp underflow ok.
    e = np.exp(sh)
    esum = float(e.sum())
    if esum <= 0: e = np.zeros(K); e[argmax]=1
    p = e / esum
    val = tmax + np.log(esum) / beta
    # grad
    r = p[::-1]
    PR = np.fft.rfft(r, L)
    H = np.fft.irfft(A * PR, L)
    start = n - 1
    end = K  # last index K-1 plus 1
    q = H[start:end][::-1]
    ga = 2.0 * float(n) * q
    s = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gx = a * (ga - s)
    gx = gx[:n-1]
    # replace nonfinite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)): gx = np.nan_to_num(gx, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    return float(val), gx
```
But c computed from rfft of length L where L≥? For score/c max, we only need L≥K. We pass L grad length ≥3n-2. Good. For objective only, L large enough. A*A length L linear? Since A is FFT of a zero-padded to L, A*A inverse gives circular convolution length L. If L≥K, first K are linear c. Good.

Gradient q uses same L≥ n+K-1 =3n-2. Good.

Should we include factor for `beta` scaling? LSE derivative: p_k = exp(beta(t_k-tmax))/sum. d val/d x = Σ p_k dt_k. We used ga from dt (val gradient) because p weights; val=LSE(t), derivative =Σ p_k dt. ga computed as 2n q: derivative of t? t=n c, d t_k / d a_j =2n a_{k-j}. Sum p -> ga. Good. val gradient wrt a =ga. No β factor. Correct.

def exact_score(a):
    n=len(a); S=a.sum(); if S<1e-300 return inf
    a=a/S? Score scale invariant; we can normalize to sum1 to avoid large.
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, L) with L=next pow K
    c=irfft(A*A,L)[:K]
    return 2*n*max(c)
```

Spectral iteration:
```
def spectral_target(a, M_target, n_steps=30, blend=True, deadline=0):
    best=a; bestscore=score
    cur=a; n=len; L=next_pow(K)
    for step in range(n_steps):
       if time>deadline: break
       A=np.fft.fft(cur, L)
       C=A*A
       c=np.fft.ifft(C).real[:K]
       # c may have tiny negatives; max with0, normalize?
       c=np.maximum(c,0)
       csum=c.sum(); if csum>0: c /= csum # should 1
       c_proj = c_waterfill(c, M_target)
       Cproj=np.fft.fft(c_proj,L)
       # make DC 1
       if Cproj[0] !=0: Cproj = Cproj / Cproj[0]
       # add tiny regularization to avoid log branch? sqrt of -0?
       Anew=np.sqrt(Cproj.astype(np.complex128))
       # ensure Anew[0] positive real
       if Anew[0].imag >0 or Anew[0].real <0: Anew = -Anew? Principal sqrt of positive real is positive; okay.
       anew=np.fft.ifft(Anew).real[:n]
       # Clip and blend
       candidates=[anew]
       for t in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]: candidates.append((1-t)*cur + t*anew)
       for cand in candidates:
           cand=np.maximum(cand,0); normalize; sc=score; if sc<bestscore update; if sc<score(cur): cur=cand? 
       # If best candidate improves cur, set cur=best_cand; else maybe break or lower M? 
    return best
```
But if M_target too low, c_proj may have flat plateau M over many bins; sqrt may yield complex A with negatives. Could be slow. We can start M_target current*0.99 and one step. Maybe just few iterations.

Because A phase half of C; multiplying by sqrt(Cproj/C) gives phase half of Cproj. This may be better when C has zeros. Compute ratio safely: `gain = np.sqrt(np.maximum(Cproj.real? complex?))`. Let's outline final code:
```
"""Docstring: ..."""
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def _pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def normalize_nonneg(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300:
       a = np.ones(len(a))*0.1
   else: a=a/s
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   return a

def conv_fft(a):
   n=len(a); K=2*n-1; L=_pow2(K)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:K]
   return c

def score_array(a):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   if len(a)==0: return np.inf
   c=conv_fft(a)
   # max should nonnegative; use max
   return float(2.0*len(a)*float(np.max(c)))

def x_from_a(a):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   n=len(a)
   # add tiny floor to avoid log0
   floor=1e-14
   safe=np.maximum(a, floor)
   safe=safe/safe.sum()
   ref=safe[-1]
   x=np.log(safe[:-1]/ref)
   # clip to avoid inf
   return np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)

def softmax_from_x(x, n):
   y=np.empty(n); y[:-1]=x; y[-1]=0.0
   m=float(np.max(y)); w=np.exp(y-m); S=w.sum()
   if S<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
   a=w/S; return a

def _obj_grad(x, n, beta, L): ...
```
In `_obj_grad`, t = n*c; c length K; if c small negative due FFT, `c = np.maximum(c,0.0)`? For gradient, if clip, derivative discontinuous at zeros; but small. Could use `c = np.where(c>0,c,0.0)`. Since c nonnegative theoretically; negative -1e-19. Clip okay.
`esum` maybe overflow? shifted ≤0 if tmax max; beta positive, exp ≤1. Underflow to0 okay. Use `np.exp(np.clip(sh, -745, 0))`.
`val = tmax + np.log(esum)/beta`. If beta very large, log esum/beta okay. If esum=0 impossible because max exp=1.
`p=e/esum`.
For gradient `H = np.fft.irfft(A * PR, L)`, A from c computation. But A length rfft (L/2+1). Multiplication valid. H length L.
`q = H[n-1:K][::-1]`. Check length K-(n-1)=2n-1 -n +1 = n. Good.
`ga=2.0*n*q`. If K large, q nonnegative? p,a nonnegative => q nonnegative. Good.
`gx = a*(ga - s)`. Note if n=1? n large. If n=1, x length0. Could handle separately: minimize? Not needed.
Return val, gx.

`run_lbfgs(a, beta, maxfun, maxiter)`:
```
def run_lbfgs(a, beta, maxfun, maxiter):
   n=len(a); L=_pow2(3*n-2)
   x0=x_from_a(a)
   opts={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxls':50}
   res=minimize(_obj_grad, x0, args=(n,beta,L), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=opts)
   a_out=softmax_from_x(res.x,n)
   return a_out
```
`minimize` may warn; okay.

return (t1-t0)/5

Implement spectral_target as top-level:
```
def spectral_target_iteration(a, M_target, n_steps, Lc, deadline):
   cur=normalize_nonneg(a); best=cur; bests=score_array(cur)
   for _ in range(n_steps):
      if time>deadline: break
      A=np.fft.fft(cur,Lc)
      C=A*A
      c=np.fft.ifft(C).real[:2*n-1]
      c=np.maximum(c,0); csum=c.sum(); if csum>0: c/=csum
      cproj=waterfill_c(c, M_target)
      Cproj=np.fft.fft(cproj,Lc)
      if Cproj[0] !=0: Cproj = Cproj / Cproj[0]
      candidates=[]
      # full sqrt
      As=np.sqrt(Cproj.astype(np.complex128))
      # symmetrize
      As=0.5*(As + np.conj(As[::-1]))
      As[0]=abs(As[0])
      candidates.append(As)
      candidates.append(-As)
      # gain from current
      eps=1e-16
      Csafe=np.where(np.abs(C)>eps, C, eps+0j)
      ratio=Cproj/Csafe
      ratio=np.where(np.abs(ratio)<1e18, ratio, 1+0j)
      gain=np.sqrt(ratio)
      Ag=A*gain
      Ag=0.5*(Ag + np.conj(Ag[::-1])); Ag[0]=abs(Ag[0])
      candidates.append(Ag)
      candidates.append(-Ag)
      cur_score=score_array(cur)
      best_cand_score=cur_score; best_cand=cur
      for Acand in candidates:
          anew=np.fft.ifft(Acand).real[:n]
          for blend in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]:
             cand=(1-blend)*cur + blend*anew
             cand=normalize_nonneg(cand)
             sc=score_array(cand)
             if sc<best_cand_score:
                 best_cand_score=sc; best_cand=cand
      if best_cand_score < bests:
          bests=best_cand_score; best=best_cand.copy()
      if best_cand_score < cur_score*0.999:
          cur=best_cand
      else:
          # if no progress, perhaps break for this M
          # could lower M? main handles
          if best_cand_score >= cur_score - 1e-12:
              # one more?
              break
   return best
```
This calls many score_array (full rfft) inside candidate evaluation. For each spectral step ~16 candidate evals. n_steps maybe 10, M stages maybe 5 -> 800 evals; okay maybe 100s. We can reduce candidates: try full sqrt, -sqrt, gain, -gain, blends 0.5,1.0 only. Good.

Waterfill function top-level:
```
def waterfill_c(c, M):
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0); s=c.sum(); if s>0: c=c/s
   K=len(c)
   if M <=0 or M < s/K? but target feasible.
   clipped=np.minimum(c, M)
   if clipped.sum() >= 1.0: # if no deficit, but max maybe >M? If M high enough, just normalize
       out=clipped.copy(); out/=out.sum(); return out
   lo=0.0; hi=max(M - float(clipped.min()), 1e-30)
   for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       val=np.sum(np.minimum(M, clipped+mid))
       if val < 1.0: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
   out=np.minimum(M, clipped+(lo+hi)/2)
   out/=out.sum()
   return out
```
If M < c.min? high. Feasible if M*K≥1. target M=0.75/n, K≈2n, M*K=1.5 >1. OK.

Mirror or active polish: Implement a simpler function `subgradient_polish(a, deadline, beta, topk)` top-level.
```
def subgradient_polish(a, deadline, beta=2000, topk=50, n_iters=2000):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a; bests=score_array(a); n=len(a); L=_pow2(3*n-2)
   step=0.05
   for it in range(n_iters):
      if time>deadline: break
      A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); c=irfft(A*A,L)[:K]; t=n*c
      # choose weights: top topk, but also exponential if beta
      if topk < K:
          idx=np.argpartition(t, -topk)[-topk:]
          tidx=t[idx]
          pidx=np.exp((tidx - tidx.max())/ (1.0/(100*n)?)) # in t units? Use beta_t = beta? t~1, beta 100.
      else: p=softmax(t*beta)
      # build full p zero and assign p normalized.
      p=np.zeros(K); p[idx]=pidx; p/=p.sum()
      r=p[::-1]; R=rfft(r,L); H=irfft(A*R,L); q=H[n-1:K][::-1]; ga=2*n*q
      d=ga - np.dot(a,ga)
      # step scaling: d_norm = np.linalg.norm(d); if small break
      # multiplicative update: a_new = a * exp(-eta * (d/d_norm?));
      # d_norm maybe ~? Use eta from step size.
      for h in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
          a_new=a*np.exp(-h*(d/ (np.linalg.norm(d)+1e-12)))
          a_new=normalize_nonneg(a_new); sc=score_array(a_new)
          if sc<bests: update; if sc<score(current): current=a_new; step=h; break
      else: maybe decrease step by 0.8
   return best
```

def random_perturb(a, rng, noise_scale):
    # multiplicative smooth noise via few Fourier modes to avoid high-freq
    n=len(a); x=np.arange(n)/n
    f1=rng.uniform(1,20); ph1=rng.uniform(0,2pi); amp1=rng.uniform(0.1,0.4)
    f2=rng.uniform(1,50); ph2=...
    mask=1+amp1*np.cos(2π f1 x+ph1)+amp2*...
    a2=a*np.maximum(mask,1e-3)
    return normalize_nonneg(a2)
```

def maybe_update(best_a, best_s, cand, label):
    cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if cand.size == 0: return best_a,best_s
    cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
    sc = score_array(cand)
    if sc < best_s:
       print(f"NEW {label}: {sc:.10f} n={cand.size}")
       return cand, sc
    return best_a,best_s
```

`factor_c_to_a(c, n, L)`:
```
def factor_c_to_a(c, n, L):
  K=2*n-1
  c=np.maximum(c,0); c/=c.sum()
  C=np.fft.fft(c,L)
  C=C/C[0]
  As=np.sqrt(C.astype(np.complex128))
  As=0.5*(As + np.conj(As[::-1])); As[0]=abs(As[0])
  a1=np.fft.ifft(As).real[:n]
  a2=np.fft.ifft(-As).real[:n]
  best=None; bestsc=np.inf
  for a in [a1,a2]:
    a=np.maximum(a,0); if a.sum()>0: a/=a.sum(); sc=score_array(a)
    if sc<bestsc: best=a; bestsc=sc
  return best
```
Try both signs. This is direct.

def optimize_spectral(a, deadline, aim_score):
   best=normalize_nonneg(a); bests=score_array(best); n=len(best)
   Lc=_pow2(2*n-1)
   cur_M = bests/(2*n)
   aim_M = aim_score/(2*n)
   # If aim_M >= cur_M, set slightly lower
   if aim_M >= cur_M: aim_M=cur_M*0.95
   M = cur_M
   fracs=[]
   while M > aim_M and len(fracs)<20:
      M = max(aim_M, M*0.98)
      fracs.append(M)
   # also include aim_M*0.99 maybe to push below target
   # For each M, run spectral_target_iteration with n_steps depending time
   for M in fracs:
      if time>deadline: break
      a_opt = spectral_target_iteration(best, M, n_steps=10, Lc=Lc, deadline=deadline)
      best,bests=maybe_update...
      M = bests/(2n) # update current M from best
   return best
```

def active_polish(a, deadline, topk=50, eta=0.1):

   sc, cur, c = score_with_c(cur); bests=sc
   L=_pow2(3*n-2); K=2*n-1

      t=n*c

      tidx=t[idx]
      pidx=np.exp(100*(tidx-tidx.max())?)

ga=grad_from_p_a(a,p,L)

Random bandlimited initial:
```
def bandlimited_sequence(n, rng, d, max_amp):
   x=np.arange(n)/n
   f=1.0
   for m in range(1,d+1):
       amp=max_amp*np.exp(-0.7*m)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       f += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x+ph)
   f=np.maximum(f,1e-3)
   return normalize_nonneg(f)
```

Let's write choose maxfun:
```
def choose_maxfun(eval_time, allow_s, cap=8000, floor=50):
    if eval_time <= 1e-6: return cap
    m = int((allow_s / eval_time) * 0.5)  # 50% evals, rest LS
    return max(floor, min(cap, m))
```
If allow_s=90, eval_time=0.01 ->4500. Good.

Resample function:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
   n=len(a)
   if n==n_new: return a.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
   out=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
   return normalize_nonneg(out)
```
This matches endpoints. If parent has edge zeros, new endpoints zero. Good.

Potential issue: `np.exp(sh)` where sh array maybe contains NaN if c NaN. c from FFT of finite a; no. If a has inf? normalize clips? `normalize_nonneg` clips? It currently max and normalize; should also `np.nan_to_num`. Implement:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(a.size if a.size>0 else 16, dtype=np.float64)/16
   a = a/s
   a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
   return a
```
If a.size=0, return ones(1)/1. But propose expects nonempty.

Implement:
```
def optimize_spectral(a, deadline, aim_score):
  best=normalize; bests=score; n=len; Lc=...
  aim_M=aim_score/(2*n); if aim_M>=bests/(2*n): aim_M=bests/(2*n)*0.9
  stalls=0
  while time<deadline:
     cur_M=bests/(2*n)
     M_next=max(aim_M, cur_M*0.98)
     stages = 10 if time remaining large else 5
     opt=spectral_target_iteration(best, M_next, stages, Lc, deadline)
     sc=score_array(opt)
     if sc < bests - 1e-10:
        best=opt; bests=sc; stalls=0
     else:
        # try a lower step
        stalls+=1
        if M_next <= aim_M*1.01:
            best, bests = spectral_target_iteration(best, aim_M, 5, Lc, deadline), score_array(...)
            break
        if stalls>=3: break
        M_next = max(aim_M, cur_M*0.95)
  return best
```
But inside if no improve, we need set next M manually. Simpler use for loop over 20 iterations: `M = max(aim_M, bests/(2n)*0.98)` each time; if no improve, reduce factor 0.95? We can keep `factor=0.98`; if stall>0 factor*=0.8.
```
factor=0.98
stall=0
for _ in range(30):
  cur_M=bests/(2n)
  M_target=max(aim_M, cur_M*factor)
  opt=...
  if improve: stall=0; factor=0.995 maybe? else stall++; factor = max(aim_M/cur_M, factor*0.7)
  if stall>=4: break
```

Let's code `active_polish` carefully top-level:
```
def active_polish(a, deadline, topk=50, eta=0.1):
    cur = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = len(cur)
    best = cur.copy()
    sc, cur, c = score_with_c(cur)
    best_s = sc
    L = _pow2(3*n - 2)
    K = 2*n - 1
    fail = 0
    min_eta = 1e-4
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        t = n * c
        if topk < K:
            idx = np.argpartition(t, -topk)[-topk:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(K)
        p = np.zeros(K)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / p.size  # but p.size? p.size=K. Use 1/len(idx)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / len(idx)
        ga = grad_from_p_a(cur, p, L)
        d = ga - float(np.dot(cur, ga))
        dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if dnorm < 1e-14: break
        new = cur * np.exp(-eta * d / dnorm)
        new = normalize_nonneg(new)
        sc2, new, c2 = score_with_c(new)
        if sc2 < sc:
            cur = new; c = c2; sc = sc2
            eta = min(0.5, eta * 1.1)
            fail = 0
        else:
            fail += 1
            eta *= 0.75
            if eta < min_eta: break
            if fail >= 8:
                fail = 0
                eta = min(0.5, eta * 2.0)
        if sc2 < best_s:
            best_s = sc2; best = new.copy()
    return best
```
But `p.size` before assignment? `p = np.zeros(K)`; `p[idx] = 1.0 / float(len(idx))`. Good.
This uses top uniform; maybe not strong. Could p weighted by (t - thresh). But okay.

`score_with_c`:
```
def score_with_c(a):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   c=conv_fft(a)
   sc=float(2.0*len(a)*float(np.max(c)))
   return sc, a, c
```

`update_with_candidate` signature:
```
def update_with_candidate(best_a, best_s, pool, cand, label):
    cand = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    if cand.size == 0:
        return best_a,best_s,pool
    sc = score_array(cand)
    if sc < best_s:
        print(f"[{label}] new best {sc:.10f} n={cand.size}")
        best_a = cand.copy(); best_s = sc
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s * 1.08 + 0.02:
        pool = add_to_pool(pool, cand, sc, 10)
    else:
        # still add best itself maybe
        pass
    return best_a,best_s,pool
```
If candidate is current best and pool empty? Initially pool added. We can just add candidate only if not too bad to keep pool small. At final if pool empty, use best.

`add_to_pool`:
```
def add_to_pool(pool, cand, sc, limit=10):
    pool = pool + [(cand.copy(), float(sc))]
    if len(pool) > limit:
        # remove worst (largest score)
        worst_idx=0; worst_val=sc
        for i, item in enumerate(pool):
            if item[1] > worst_val:
                worst_val=item[1]; worst_idx=i
        pool = pool[:worst_idx] + pool[worst_idx+1:]
    return pool
```

```python
"""
Non-negative step-function convolution peak minimizer.

Strategy:
1. Use the previous construction as the primary seed.
2. Build a few diverse spectral / target-envelope initializations.
3. Polish with:
   - alternating spectral square-root / gain projection against water-filled
     convolution envelopes,
   - L-BFGS-B on a softmax-simplex parameterization using LSE-smooth maximum
     of the self-convolution,
   - active-peak active-gradient mirror polishing,
   - one careful upsampling attempt if time remains.
4. Keep a small pool of strong candidates and, near the end, verify them with
   evaluate_sequence and return the verified best.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _pow2(m):
    """Next power of two >= m."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Clip to non-negative, normalize to sum 1, and make safe."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def conv_fft(a):
    """Linear self-convolution by FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    k = 2 * n - 1
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([])
    L = _pow2(k)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:k]
    return c


def score_array(a):
    """Fast local score: 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a)^2)."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = conv_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def score_with_c(a):
    """Return score, normalized a, and convolution."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    c = conv_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf, a, np.array([])
    sc = float(2.0 * a.size * float(np.max(c)))
    return sc, a, c


def x_from_a(a):
    """Log-coordinates for softmax with last coordinate fixed to 0."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    floor = 1e-14
    safe = np.maximum(a, floor)
    safe = safe / safe.sum()
    ref = safe[-1]
    if ref <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ref):
        ref = 1e-14
    x = np.log(safe[:-1] / ref)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    return np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)


def softmax_from_x(x, n):
    """Inverse of x_from_a."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n - 1:
        x = np.resize(x, n - 1)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[:-1] = x
    y[-1] = 0.0
    m = float(np.max(y))
    w = np.exp(y - m)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = w / s
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def objective_grad(x, n, beta, L):
    """
    LSE-smooth objective and gradient on the simplex.

    Objective is F = LSE_beta(t), t_k = n * c_k, c = conv(a,a), sum(a)=1.
    Returning F (without factor 2) is equivalent for optimization.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    a = softmax_from_x(x, n)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    L = int(L)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:K]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    t = n * c
    tmax = float(np.max(t))
    sh = beta * (t - tmax)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(sh)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(es) or es <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(t))] = 1.0
        es = 1.0
        e = p
    p = e / es

    val = tmax + float(np.log(es)) / float(beta)

    # Gradient of t w.r.t. a:
    # d t_k / d a_j = 2n * a_{k-j} when valid.
    r = p[::-1]
    PR = np.fft.rfft(r, L)
    H = np.fft.irfft(A * PR, L)
    q = H[n - 1:K][::-1]
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    ga = 2.0 * float(n) * q

    s = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        s = 0.0
    gx = a * (ga - s)
    gx = gx[:n - 1]
    gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
    return float(val), gx.astype(np.float64)


def run_lbfgs_stage(a, beta, maxfun):
    """Run one L-BFGS stage on softmax coordinates."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    L = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    x0 = x_from_a(a)
    maxfun = max(50, int(maxfun))
    opts = {
        "maxiter": max(100, maxfun),
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxls": 50,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(objective_grad, x0, args=(n, beta, L),
                       method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, options=opts)
        xout = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        xout = np.where(np.isfinite(xout), xout, 0.0)
        return softmax_from_x(xout, n)
    except Exception:
        return a


def measure_eval_time(a, beta=1000):
    """Rough runtime of one objective+gradient evaluation."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return 1e-3
    L = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    x = x_from_a(a)
    try:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        for _ in range(3):
            objective_grad(x, n, beta, L)
        dt = time.monotonic() - t0
        if dt <= 0.0:
            dt = 1e-3
        return float(dt / 3.0)
    except Exception:
        return 1e-2


def choose_maxfun(eval_time, allow_s, cap=8000):
    if eval_time is None or eval_time <= 1e-6:
        return cap
    m = int((allow_s / max(eval_time, 1e-6)) * 0.55)
    return max(50, min(int(cap), m))


def optimize_lbfgs_sequence(a, deadline, eval_time=None):
    """Multi-beta L-BFGS polishing."""
    best = normalize_nonneg(a)
    bests = score_array(best)
    n = best.size
    if n <= 1:
        return best

    if eval_time is None:
        eval_time = measure_eval_time(best, 1000)

    # Alternating smooth/sharp LSE stages.
    betas = [100, 1000, 300, 3000, 1000, 10000, 3000, 30000, 10000, 100000]
    stalls = 0

    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        tnow = time.monotonic()
        if tnow >= deadline:
            break
        remaining = deadline - tnow
        if remaining < 25.0:
            break

        allow = min(80.0, max(15.0, remaining * 0.10))
        cap = 12000 if beta < 1000 else 6000
        maxfun = choose_maxfun(eval_time, allow, cap=cap)
        if maxfun < 80:
            break

        cand = run_lbfgs_stage(best, beta, maxfun)
        sc = score_array(cand)
        if sc < bests - 1e-11:
            best = cand.copy()
            bests = sc
            stalls = 0
            # Recompute a fresh eval-time estimate occasionally.
            if idx % 3 == 0:
                eval_time = measure_eval_time(best, beta)
        else:
            stalls += 1
            if stalls >= 4:
                break
    return best


def waterfill_c(c, M):
    """Clip c to cap M and raise low values until sum is 1."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
    c = c / s
    M = float(M)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return c

    clipped = np.minimum(c, M)
    scp = float(clipped.sum())
    if scp >= 0.999999999999:
        out = clipped.copy()
        out /= out.sum()
        return out

    lo = 0.0
    hi = max(M - float(clipped.min()), 1e-30)
    if hi <= 0.0:
        hi = 1e-30

    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = np.sum(np.minimum(M, clipped + mid))
        if val < 1.0:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    out = np.minimum(M, clipped + 0.5 * (lo + hi))
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out = clipped.copy()
        s = float(out.sum())
    out /= s
    return out


def factor_c_to_a(c, n, L):
    """Spectral square-root factor of target convolution, with sign search."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    K = 2 * n - 1
    if c.size != K:
        c = np.resize(c, K)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        c = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
    else:
        c = c / s

    try:
        C = np.fft.fft(c, L).astype(np.complex128)
        if abs(C[0]) > 1e-15:
            C = C / C[0]

        As = np.sqrt(C)
        # Enforce Hermitian symmetry for a real inverse FFT.
        As = 0.5 * (As + np.conj(As[::-1]))
        As[0] = abs(As[0])

        best_a = None
        best_sc = np.inf
        for sign in (1, -1):
            anew = np.fft.ifft(sign * As).real[:n]
            anew = np.where(np.isfinite(anew), anew, 0.0)
            anew = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
            anew = normalize_nonneg(anew)
            sc = score_array(anew)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = anew.copy()
        return best_a
    except Exception:
        return None


def spectral_target_iteration(a, M_target, n_steps, L, deadline):
    """
    Alternate:
      A^2 -> time c -> water-filled target c_proj -> new A via sqrt/gain -> a.
    """
    cur = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = cur.size
    best = cur.copy()
    bests = score_array(cur)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    cur_score = bests

    for _ in range(int(n_steps)):
        tnow = time.monotonic()
        if tnow >= deadline:
            break

        try:
            A = np.fft.fft(cur, L)
            C = A * A
            c = np.fft.ifft(C).real[:K]
            c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            cs = float(c.sum())
            if cs <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cs):
                break
            c = c / cs

            cproj = waterfill_c(c, M_target)
            Cproj = np.fft.fft(cproj, L).astype(np.complex128)
            if abs(Cproj[0]) > 1e-15:
                Cproj = Cproj / Cproj[0]

            candidates = []

            # Direct square root of target spectrum.
            As = np.sqrt(Cproj)
            As = 0.5 * (As + np.conj(As[::-1]))
            As[0] = abs(As[0])
            candidates.append(As)
            candidates.append(-As)

            # Multiplicative gain from current A.
            absC = np.abs(C)
            ratio = np.ones_like(Cproj)
            ok = absC > 1e-18
            ratio[ok] = Cproj[ok] / C[ok]
            ratio = np.where(np.abs(ratio) < 1e12, ratio, 1.0 + 0.0j)
            gain = np.sqrt(ratio)
            Ag = A * gain
            Ag = 0.5 * (Ag + np.conj(Ag[::-1]))
            Ag[0] = abs(Ag[0])
            candidates.append(Ag)

            cur_candidate_score = cur_score
            cur_candidate = cur

            for Acand in candidates:
                anew = np.fft.ifft(Acand).real[:n]
                anew = np.where(np.isfinite(anew), anew, 0.0)
                for blend in (0.5, 1.0):
                    cand = (1.0 - blend) * cur + blend * anew
                    cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
                    sc = score_array(cand)
                    if sc < cur_candidate_score:
                        cur_candidate_score = sc
                        cur_candidate = cand
                    if sc < bests:
                        bests = sc
                        best = cand.copy()

            if cur_candidate_score < cur_score - 1e-12:
                cur = cur_candidate.copy()
                cur_score = cur_candidate_score
            else:
                break
        except Exception:
            break

    return best


def optimize_spectral(a, deadline, aim_score):
    """Repeated spectral envelope projection toward aim_score."""
    best = normalize_nonneg(a)
    bests = score_array(best)
    n = best.size
    if n <= 1:
        return best

    L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    cur_M = bests / (2.0 * float(n))
    aim_M = float(aim_score) / (2.0 * float(n))
    if aim_M >= cur_M:
        aim_M = cur_M * 0.90

    factor = 0.98
    stall = 0

    for _ in range(35):
        tnow = time.monotonic()
        if tnow >= deadline:
            break

        remaining = deadline - tnow
        if remaining < 30.0:
            break

        cur_M = bests / (2.0 * float(n))
        M_target = max(aim_M, cur_M * factor)

        if remaining > 900.0:
            n_steps = 12
        elif remaining > 300.0:
            n_steps = 7
        else:
            n_steps = 4

        cand = spectral_target_iteration(best, M_target, n_steps, L, deadline)
        sc = score_array(cand)

        if sc < bests - 1e-11:
            best = cand.copy()
            bests = sc
            stall = 0
            # If making progress, use slightly gentler reductions.
            factor = 0.99
        else:
            stall += 1
            if M_target <= aim_M * 1.005:
                # Try aim directly one last time.
                cand2 = spectral_target_iteration(best, aim_M, 5, L, deadline)
                sc2 = score_array(cand2)
                if sc2 < bests - 1e-11:
                    best = cand2.copy()
                    bests = sc2
                break
            factor = max(aim_M / max(cur_M, 1e-30), factor * 0.75)
            if stall >= 5:
                break

    return best


def grad_from_p_a(a, p, L):
    """Gradient 2n * sum_k p_k a_{k-j}."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    K = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size != K:
        p = np.resize(p, K)
    r = p[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    PR = np.fft.rfft(r, L)
    H = np.fft.irfft(A * PR, L)
    q = H[n - 1:K][::-1]
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * float(n) * q


def active_polish(a, deadline, topk=50, eta=0.1):
    """Active-peak subgradient mirror polishing."""
    cur = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = cur.size
    if n <= 1:
        return cur
    sc, cur, c = score_with_c(cur)
    best = cur.copy()
    bests = sc
    L = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    eta = float(eta)
    fail = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        t = n * c
        if topk < K:
            idx = np.argpartition(t, -topk)[-topk:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(K)
        if idx.size == 0:
            break

        p = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        ga = grad_from_p_a(cur, p, L)
        d = ga - float(np.dot(cur, ga))
        d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
        dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if dnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        new = cur * np.exp(-eta * d / dnorm)
        new = normalize_nonneg(new)
        sc2, new, c2 = score_with_c(new)

        if sc2 < sc:
            cur = new
            c = c2
            sc = sc2
            eta = min(0.6, eta * 1.1)
            fail = 0
        else:
            fail += 1
            eta *= 0.78
            if fail >= 9:
                fail = 0
                eta = min(0.6, eta * 2.0)
            if eta < 1e-4:
                break

        if sc2 < bests:
            bests = sc2
            best = new.copy()

    return best


def bandlimited_sequence(n, rng, d, max_amp):
    """Positive smooth random band-limited initializer."""
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, int(d) + 1):
        amp = max_amp * np.exp(-0.75 * float(m))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        fr = rng.uniform(0.8, 1.3)
        f += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * fr * float(m) * x + ph)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-5)
    return normalize_nonneg(f)


def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    """Linearly resample a normalized sequence to a new length."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n_old = a.size
    if n_new <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def add_to_pool(pool, cand, sc, limit=10):
    """Pool helper: keep a small handful of good candidates."""
    sc = float(sc)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = np.inf
    pool = list(pool) + [(np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).copy(), sc)]
    if len(pool) > limit:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_val = pool[0][1]
        for i, item in enumerate(pool):
            if item[1] > worst_val:
                worst_val = item[1]
                worst_idx = i
        pool = pool[:worst_idx] + pool[worst_idx + 1:]
    return pool


def update_with_candidate(best_a, best_s, pool, cand, label):
    """Evaluate candidate locally; update best and pool."""
    cand = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    if cand.size == 0:
        return best_a, best_s, pool
    sc = score_array(cand)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best_a, best_s, pool
    if sc < best_s:
        print(f"[{label}] new local best = {sc:.10f}, n={cand.size}")
        best_a = cand.copy()
        best_s = sc
    # Keep promising candidates only.
    if sc < best_s * 1.06 + 0.02:
        pool = add_to_pool(pool, cand, sc, 10)
    return best_a, best_s, pool


def make_output_list(arr):
    """Convert to a valid Python list for evaluate_sequence."""
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.011:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    arr = arr / s
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def final_select(pool, default_a, default_s, time_budget):
    """Verify a small pool with evaluate_sequence; return best verified list."""
    time_budget = max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    best_list = make_output_list(default_a)
    best_val = float(default_s)

    try:
        v = evaluate_sequence(best_list)
        if np.isfinite(v):
            best_val = float(v)
    except Exception:
        pass

    pool = list(pool)
    large_count = 0

    for i, item in enumerate(pool):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_budget:
            break
        if i >= 12:
            break

        cand, internal = item
        if not np.isfinite(internal):
            continue
        # If this candidate is clearly worse than current verified best, skip.
        if np.isfinite(best_val) and internal > best_val + 0.005:
            continue

        L = len(cand)
        if L > 12000:
            if large_count >= 2:
                break
            large_count += 1

        lst = make_output_list(cand)
        try:
            v = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        except Exception:
            v = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(v) and float(v) < best_val:
            best_val = float(v)
            best_list = lst

    return best_list, best_val


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing the evaluation function.
    Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    reserve = 60.0
    active_total = max(10.0, total - reserve)
    deadline = t0 + active_total

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    target_bound = 1.5027

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        parent_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if parent_obj is not None:
            p = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size >= 32:
                parent = normalize_nonneg(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
        parent = normalize_nonneg(parent)

    best_a = parent.copy()
    best_s = score_array(best_a)
    pool = add_to_pool([], best_a, best_s, 10)

    print(f"Start: n={best_a.size}, local score={best_s:.10f}")

    # Dynamic aim: try to beat target by a small margin, or improve current.
    def calc_aim():
        # No nested functions in final code, so inline this as a local variable
        # in each phase instead of a nested function.
        pass

    aim_score = min(target_bound - 0.002, max(1.49, best_s - 0.002))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial diverse candidates.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    n = best_a.size
    cands = []
    cands.append(best_a)

    # Smooth asymmetric masks.
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    masks = [
        1.0 + 0.55 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        1.0 - 0.55 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        1.0 + 0.35 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        1.0 + 0.45 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x - 1.0),
    ]
    for mi, m in enumerate(masks):
        cand = np.maximum(best_a * m, 1e-8)
        cands.append(normalize_nonneg(cand))

    # Spectral target-envelope initializations.
    cur_M0 = best_s / (2.0 * float(n))
    aim_M0 = aim_score / (2.0 * float(n))
    if aim_M0 < cur_M0:
        c0 = conv_fft(best_a)
        for f in (0.995, 0.99, 0.98, aim_M0 / max(cur_M0, 1e-30)):
            M = max(aim_M0, cur_M0 * f)
            cproj = waterfill_c(c0, M)
            cand = factor_c_to_a(cproj, n, _pow2(2 * n - 1))
            if cand is not None:
                cands.append(cand)

    # A few random smooth shapes.
    for d, amp in ((2, 0.35), (3, 0.45), (4, 0.55)):
        cands.append(bandlimited_sequence(n, rng, d, amp))

    for ci, cand in enumerate(cands):
        best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(
            best_a, best_s, pool, cand, f"init{ci}")
    aim_score = min(target_bound - 0.002, max(1.49, best_s - 0.002))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: spectral envelope projection.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    phase1_deadline = t0 + 0.22 * active_total
    if time.monotonic() < phase1_deadline:
        cand = optimize_spectral(best_a, phase1_deadline, aim_score)
        best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(
            best_a, best_s, pool, cand, "spec1")
        aim_score = min(target_bound - 0.002, max(1.49, best_s - 0.002))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: primary L-BFGS polish.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    phase2_deadline = t0 + 0.58 * active_total
    if time.monotonic() < phase2_deadline:
        eval_time = measure_eval_time(best_a, 1000)
        cand = optimize_lbfgs_sequence(best_a, phase2_deadline, eval_time)
        best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(
            best_a, best_s, pool, cand, "lbfgs1")
        aim_score = min(target_bound - 0.002, max(1.49, best_s - 0.002))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: spectral projection again.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    phase3_deadline = t0 + 0.75 * active_total
    if time.monotonic() < phase3_deadline:
        cand = optimize_spectral(best_a, phase3_deadline, aim_score)
        best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(
            best_a, best_s, pool, cand, "spec2")
        aim_score = min(target_bound - 0.002, max(1.49, best_s - 0.002))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 4: active-peak polishing.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    phase4_deadline = t0 + 0.87 * active_total
    if time.monotonic() < phase4_deadline:
        cand = active_polish(best_a, phase4_deadline, topk=50, eta=0.10)
        best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(
            best_a, best_s, pool, cand, "active")
        aim_score = min(target_bound - 0.002, max(1.49, best_s - 0.002))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 5: one careful upsampling attempt if we still need it.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0 and best_s > target_bound * 0.9995:
        ncur = best_a.size
        candidates_len = []
        for nl in (8192, 10240, 12288, 16384, ncur * 2, ncur + 1536):
            if isinstance(nl, float):
                nl = int(round(nl))
            if nl > ncur and 4096 <= nl <= 16384 and nl not in candidates_len:
                candidates_len.append(nl)

        for n2 in candidates_len[:2]:
            tnow = time.monotonic()
            if tnow > deadline - 160.0:
                break

            a2 = resample_sequence(best_a, n2)
            best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(
                best_a, best_s, pool, a2, f"upsample{n2}")

            # Target envelope at the new length.
            try:
                c2 = conv_fft(a2)
                M2 = aim_score / (2.0 * float(n2))
                if M2 < best_s / (2.0 * float(n2)):
                    cproj2 = waterfill_c(c2, M2)
                    a3 = factor_c_to_a(cproj2, n2, _pow2(2 * n2 - 1))
                    if a3 is not None:
                        best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(
                            best_a, best_s, pool, a3, f"upsample-fac{n2}")
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Short optimization at new length.
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 120.0:
                eval_time2 = measure_eval_time(best_a, 1000)
                spec_end = min(deadline - 80.0, time.monotonic() + min(120.0, rem * 0.35))
                if spec_end > time.monotonic() + 20.0:
                    candspec = optimize_spectral(best_a, spec_end, aim_score)
                    best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(
                        best_a, best_s, pool, candspec, f"upspec{n2}")
                    eval_time2 = measure_eval_time(best_a, 1000)

                lb_end = min(deadline - 40.0, time.monotonic() + min(220.0, rem * 0.55))
                if lb_end > time.monotonic() + 20.0:
                    candlb = optimize_lbfgs_sequence(best_a, lb_end, eval_time2)
                    best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(
                        best_a, best_s, pool, candlb, f"uplb{n2}")

            aim_score = min(target_bound - 0.002, max(1.49, best_s - 0.002))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final lightweight L-BFGS if time remains and we are close.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        eval_time = measure_eval_time(best_a, 3000)
        cand = optimize_lbfgs_sequence(best_a, deadline - 20.0, eval_time)
        best_a, best_s, pool = update_with_candidate(best_a, best_s, pool, cand, "final-lbfgs")

    # Convert to valid list and verify a small final pool.
    final_deadline_hard = t0 + total - 3.0
    verify_budget = min(30.0, max(5.0, final_deadline_hard - time.monotonic()))
    out_list, verified = final_select(pool, best_a, best_s, verify_budget)

    if not out_list:
        out_list = make_output_list(best_a)
        verified = np.nan

    out_list = make_output_list(out_list)
    print(f"FINAL selected: local best={best_s:.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}, n={len(out_list)}")
    return out_list
```
